\lrcorner$}}A]^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\beta}}}}{[\bbvar{X}\text{\large{$\lrcorner$}}A]^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\beta}}}}\mathchoice{\Lambda^{{{\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}}}}{\Lambda^{{{\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}}}}{\Lambda^{{{\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}}}}{\Lambda^{{{\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}}}}=\bbvar{d}\mathchoice{\Lambda^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}}}}{\Lambda^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}}}}{\Lambda^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}}}}{\Lambda^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}}}}-\nabla_{\bbvar{X}}\mathchoice{\Lambda^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}}}}{\Lambda^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}}}}{\Lambda^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}}}}{\Lambda^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}}}}.

(54)

The covariant field space differentials
\bbvar{D}e^{\alpha}
and
\bbvar{D}A^{\alpha}_{\;\;\beta}
encode the
physical
variations of the tetrad and connection fields, that is, those variations that cannot be undone by simply performing an appropriate diffeomorphism or internal gauge transformation on the transformed fields.

Inserting (
52
) and (
53
) into (
48
) and using that
\mathcal{L}{X}A=\bbvar{X}\text{\large{$\lrcorner$}}F
, we obtain a bulk and a boundary term for the pre-symplectic form:

\Omega_{\Sigma}=\Omega_{\Sigma}^{\text{bulk}}+\Omega_{\partial\Sigma}.

(55)

The bulk term is

\displaystyle\Omega_{\Sigma}^{\text{bulk}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{16\pi G}\int_{\Sigma}\bigg[\bbvar{D}\Pi_{\alpha\beta}\wedge\bbvar{D}A^{\alpha\beta}+2\bbvar{X}_{\alpha}\bbvar{D}H^{\alpha}+(\bbvar{X}\text{\large{$\lrcorner$}}G_{\alpha\beta})\wedge\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\bbvar{D}A+\bbvar{X}\text{\large{$\lrcorner$}}F}}\right)^{\alpha\beta}

\displaystyle\qquad-2(\bbvar{D}e_{\alpha}+\nabla\bbvar{X}_{\alpha})\wedge\bbvar{X}\text{\large{$\lrcorner$}}H^{\alpha}-2T_{\alpha}\bbvar{X}_{\beta}\wedge\bbvar{X}\text{\large{$\lrcorner$}}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left[(\ast-\gamma^{-1})F}}\right]^{\alpha\beta}

\displaystyle\qquad-G_{\alpha\beta}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\bbvar{D}\Lambda\Lambda^{-1}\bbvar{D}\Lambda\Lambda^{-1}}}\right)^{\alpha\beta}+\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\bbvar{D}G_{\alpha\beta}+\mathcal{L}_{\bbvar{X}}G_{\alpha\beta}}}\right)\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\bbvar{D}\Lambda\Lambda^{-1}}}\right)^{\alpha\beta}\bigg],

(56)

where
\bbvar{X}_{\alpha}:=\bbvar{X}\text{\large{$\lrcorner$}}e_{\alpha}
and

\displaystyle H^{\alpha}

\displaystyle=\mathchoice{\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left[(\ast-\gamma^{-1})F}}\right]^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\beta}}}}{\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left[(\ast-\gamma^{-1})F}}\right]^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\beta}}}}{\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left[(\ast-\gamma^{-1})F}}\right]^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\beta}}}}{\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left[(\ast-\gamma^{-1})F}}\right]^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\beta}}}}\wedge e^{\beta}\approx 0,

(57)

\displaystyle T_{\alpha}

\displaystyle=\nabla e_{\alpha}\approx 0,

(58)

\displaystyle G_{\alpha\beta}

\displaystyle=\nabla\Pi_{\alpha\beta}=2\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\tfrac{1}{2}\mathchoice{\epsilon^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.9061pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21864pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\alpha^{\prime}\beta^{\prime}}}}_{{{\alpha\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[13.42815pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[13.42815pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.83109pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[7.14363pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\epsilon^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.9061pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21864pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\alpha^{\prime}\beta^{\prime}}}}_{{{\alpha\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[13.42815pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[13.42815pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.83109pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[7.14363pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\epsilon^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.9061pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21864pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\alpha^{\prime}\beta^{\prime}}}}_{{{\alpha\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[13.42815pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[13.42815pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.83109pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[7.14363pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\epsilon^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.73317pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.9061pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.21864pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\alpha^{\prime}\beta^{\prime}}}}_{{{\alpha\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[13.42815pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[13.42815pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[8.83109pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[7.14363pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}-\gamma^{-1}\delta_{[\alpha}^{\alpha^{\prime}}\delta_{\beta]}^{\beta^{\prime}}}}\right)\nabla e_{\alpha^{\prime}}\wedge e_{\beta^{\prime}}\approx 0

(59)

denote the constraints. The symbol
\approx
indicates that the constraints vanish only on-shell, i.e. upon imposing the Einstein equations. Note that on-shell, the bulk term reduces to the simple canonical form

\Omega_{\Sigma}^{\text{bulk}}\approx\frac{1}{16\pi G}\int_{\Sigma}\bbvar{D}\Pi_{\alpha\beta}\wedge\bbvar{D}A^{\alpha\beta}.

(60)

Then, we also have a boundary contribution to the pre-symplectic form. It is

\displaystyle\Omega_{\partial\Sigma}=\frac{1}{16\pi G}\oint_{\partial\Sigma}\bigg[

\displaystyle\Pi_{\alpha\beta}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\bbvar{D}\Lambda\Lambda^{-1}\bbvar{D}\Lambda\Lambda^{-1}}}\right)^{\alpha\beta}-\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\bbvar{D}\Pi+\mathcal{L}_{\bbvar{X}}\Pi}}\right)_{\alpha\beta}(\bbvar{D}\Lambda\Lambda^{-1})^{\alpha\beta}

\displaystyle+(\bbvar{X}\text{\large{$\lrcorner$}}\Pi_{\alpha\beta})\wedge\bbvar{D}A^{\alpha\beta}+\bbvar{X}_{\alpha}\bbvar{X}_{\beta}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left((\ast-\gamma^{-1})F}}\right)^{\alpha\beta}\bigg].

(61)

This boundary contribution encodes the symplectic structure of the reference fields
X^{\mu}
and
\Lambda^{\alpha}_{\;\;\mu}
and their conjugate momenta. Since these fields enter the symplectic two-form through a boundary term, they are referred to as
edge
or
boundary modes
(e.g.
[
35
,
57
]
) on the covariant phase space. While they encode only gauge-redundant information in the bulk, they become physical at the boundary. That is, to obtain a full description of the subsystem, it is not enough to specify the radiative degrees of freedom alone. In addition, we also need to specify the configuration of the reference fields and their conjugate momenta at the corner
\partial\Sigma
.

III.2
Linearized Theory

Above, we introduced reference frames for covariant gauge fixing conditions using covariant and canonical phase space techniques. The goal of this section is to connect our construction to the more standard procedure in linearized gravity. Thus, we take linearized perturbations around a flat spacetime background
(^{(0)}e^{\alpha},^{(0)}\mathchoice{A^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\beta}}}}{A^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\beta}}}}{A^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\beta}}}}{A^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\beta}}}})
. It is important to note that we do not distinguish at this stage between gauge variations and the variations of the true, albeit linearized, physical degrees of freedom. Therefore, we include perturbations of the clock variables around a fiducial background configuration
X_{o}^{\mu}
. We thus write

X^{\mu}=X^{\mu}_{o}+\delta X^{\mu}+\mathcal{O}(\delta^{2}).

(62)

In the same way, we also include perturbations of the internal reference fields
\mathchoice{\Lambda^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}}}}{\Lambda^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}}}}{\Lambda^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}}}}{\Lambda^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}}}}(X)
. Notice that the Lorentz frames carry an explicit dependence on the coordinates. Linearizing both
\Lambda^{\alpha}{}_{\mu}
and
X^{\mu}
will have a combined effect. There are variations
\delta X^{\mu}
of the coordinates alone and variations that only change the Lorentz reference frames while keeping
X^{\mu}
fixed. We encode variations of
\Lambda(X)
that do not result from changing
X^{\mu}
into an
\mathfrak{so}(1,3)

\displaystyle={}^{(0)}\mathchoice{\Lambda^{{{\alpha^{\prime}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.04625pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.04625pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.59703pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.70145pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}}}}{\Lambda^{{{\alpha^{\prime}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.04625pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.04625pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.59703pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.70145pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}}}}{\Lambda^{{{\alpha^{\prime}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.04625pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.04625pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.59703pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.70145pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}}}}{\Lambda^{{{\alpha^{\prime}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.04625pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.04625pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.59703pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.70145pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}}}}{}^{(0)}\mathchoice{\Lambda^{{{\beta^{\prime}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.3819pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.3819pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.23405pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.44218pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}{\Lambda^{{{\beta^{\prime}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.3819pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.3819pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.23405pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.44218pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}{\Lambda^{{{\beta^{\prime}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.3819pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.3819pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.23405pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.44218pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}{\Lambda^{{{\beta^{\prime}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.3819pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.3819pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.23405pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.44218pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu}}}}\underset{\equiv\delta G^{\mu\nu}}{\underbrace{\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\epsilon_{\alpha\beta\alpha^{\prime}\beta^{\prime}}-2\gamma^{-1}\delta_{\alpha^{\prime}[\alpha}\delta_{\beta]\beta^{\prime}}}}\right)\delta T^{\mu}\wedge\mathrm{d}X_{o}^{\nu}}+\mathcal{O}(\delta^{2})}.

(79)

Finally, we have all the ingredients at hand to compute the perturbative expansion of the pre-symplectic two-form
\Omega_{\Sigma}
, which we introduced in the previous section at the non-perturbative level. First, we evaluate
\Omega_{\Sigma}
at a configuration, which is flat—that is, we consider the pre-symplectic form for the tangent space
T_{\mathcalvar{p}_{o}}\mathcal{P}
around a point
\mathcalvar{p}_{o}\in\mathcal{P}
in phase space that describes a flat configuration
\mathcalvar{p}_{o}=({}^{(0)}e^{\alpha},{}^{(0)}\mathchoice{A^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\beta}}}}{A^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\beta}}}}{A^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\beta}}}}{A^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\beta}}}})
as in (
69
) above. We restrict ourselves to a flat solution, while imposing no constraints on the variations
\bbvar{d}e^{\alpha}
,
\bbvar{d}\mathchoice{A^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\beta}}}}{A^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\beta}}}}{A^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\beta}}}}{A^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\beta}}}}
of the fundamental fields—thus exploring all directions
T_{\mathcalvar{p}_{o}}\mathcal{P}
of tangent space for
\mathcalvar{p}_{o}
fixed. We obtain

\displaystyle\Omega_{\Sigma}\Big|_{\mathcalvar{p}_{0}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{16\pi G}\int_{\Sigma}\bigg[\bbvar{D}\Pi_{\alpha\beta}\wedge\bbvar{D}A^{\alpha\beta}+2\bbvar{X}_{\alpha}\bbvar{D}H^{\alpha}+\bbvar{D}G_{\alpha\beta}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\bbvar{D}\Lambda\Lambda^{-1}}}\right)^{\alpha\beta}\bigg]

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{16\pi G}\oint_{\partial\Sigma}\bigg[\Pi_{\alpha\beta}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\bbvar{D}\Lambda\Lambda^{-1}\bbvar{D}\Lambda\Lambda^{-1}}}\right)^{\alpha\beta}

(80)

\displaystyle-\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\bbvar{D}\Pi+\mathcal{L}_{\bbvar{X}}\Pi}}\right)_{\alpha\beta}(\bbvar{D}\Lambda\Lambda^{-1})^{\alpha\beta}+(\bbvar{X}\text{\large{$\lrcorner$}}\Pi_{\alpha\beta})\wedge\bbvar{D}A^{\alpha\beta}\bigg].

Next, we insert the perturbative expansion back into (
80
). This way, we obtain the first-order contribution to the pre-symplectic form,
{}^{(1)}\Omega_{\Sigma}
. We identify three terms:

{}^{(1)}\Omega_{\Sigma}={}^{(1)}\Omega_{\Sigma}^{\text{rad}}+{}^{(1)}\Omega_{\Sigma}^{\text{diff}}+{}^{(1)}\Omega_{\Sigma}^{\text{Lorentz}}.

(81)

The first term describes the radiative modes at the linearized level. It consists of the bulk integral

{}^{(1)}\Omega_{\Sigma}^{\text{rad}}=\frac{1}{8\pi G}\int_{\Sigma}\mathrm{d}X_{o}^{[\mu}\wedge\bbvar{d}(\delta f^{\nu]})\big[(\ast-\gamma^{-1})\bbvar{d}(\delta\Delta)\big]_{\mu\nu}.

(82)

Next, we have the contribution from the reference fields for the diffeomorphisms. At the linearized level, these are the perturbations
\delta X^{\mu}
of the coordinate fields. The resulting contribution to the total pre-symplectic two-form is a sum of two terms:

{}^{(1)}\Omega_{\Sigma}^{\text{diff}}=\frac{1}{8\pi G}\int_{\Sigma}\bbvar{d}(\delta X^{\mu})\,\bbvar{d}(\delta H_{\mu})-\oint_{\partial\Sigma}\bbvar{d}(\delta X^{\mu})\,\bbvar{d}(\delta\bm{P}_{\mu}).

(83)

The first term, which is a bulk integral, vanishes once the constraints are imposed. On-shell, the only non-zero contribution comes from the boundary term, which allows us to identify the
momentum current

\delta\bm{P}_{\mu}
conjugate to the first-order linearized coordinate fields,

\delta\bm{P}_{\mu}=\frac{1}{8\pi G}\big[(\ast-\gamma^{-1})\delta\Delta\big]_{\mu\nu}\wedge\mathrm{d}X^{\nu}_{o}.

(84)

From (
83
), we conclude that
X^{\mu}
is indeed a reference frame for the constraint, i.e.
\{X^{\mu}(q),H_{\nu}(q^{\prime})\}\sim\delta^{\mu}_{\nu}\delta^{(3)}(q,q^{\prime})
in analogy to (
38
). This does not imply, however, that the reference frames also Poisson commute among themselves. Instead, we have

\big\{\delta X^{\mu}(q),\delta X^{\nu}(q^{\prime})\big\}=\Theta^{\mu\nu}(q,q^{\prime}),

(85)

for some bi-tensor field
\Theta^{\mu\nu}(q,q^{\prime})
that does not vanish on the constraint hypersurface
H_{\mu}=0
,
G_{\mu\nu}=0
. This non-commutativity has a simple geometric origin. The field space covariant derivative
\bbvar{D}
introduced in (
52
) and (
53
) removes the gauge variations from the functional derivative of the fundamental fields. It does not remove, however, transversal variations that take us away from the constraint hypersurface
H_{\mu}=0,G_{\mu\nu}=0
. This has an important consequence. Consider coordinates
(a_{\pm},\bar{a}_{\pm},H_{\mu},X^{\mu},\mathchoice{\Lambda^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}}}}{\Lambda^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}}}}{\Lambda^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}}}}{\Lambda^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}}}})
in a vicinity of the constraint hypersurface such that
(a_{\pm},\bar{a}_{\pm})
parametrize the two modes of gravitational radiation thereon.
6
6

6

In linearized gravity, we can access these modes via a standard projector onto e.g. the transverse and traceless modes.
Since
\bbvar{D}
removes the pure gauge variations from the exterior derivative, we have

\bbvar{D}=\int_{\Sigma}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left[\sum_{s=\pm}\Big(\bbvar{d}a_{s}\frac{\delta}{\delta a_{s}}+\bbvar{d}a_{s}\frac{\delta}{\delta\bar{a}_{s}}\Big)+\bbvar{d}H_{\mu}\frac{\delta}{\delta H_{\mu}}+\bbvar{d}G_{\mu\nu}\frac{\delta}{\delta G_{\mu\nu}}}}\right].

(86)

If we now return to (
56
), we see that for any such choice of
(a_{\pm},\bar{a}_{\pm})
phase space coordinates the momentum conjugate to
H_{\mu}
cannot be the reference frame
X^{\mu}
alone. At the linearized level, it is simply
X^{\mu}
shifted by terms linear in the constraints. Since the constraints do not commute with the reference fields, this in turn creates the non-commutativity of the embedding fields
X^{\mu}
. We will comment on this non-commutativity for linearized clocks in Subsection
IV.1
below.

Finally, there is the symplectic structure for the internal Lorentz reference frames,

{}^{(1)}\Omega_{\Sigma}^{\text{Lorentz}}=\frac{1}{16\pi G}\int_{\Sigma}\bbvar{d}(\delta G_{\mu\nu})\,\bbvar{d}(\delta{\mkern 0.8mu\mathchar 22\mkern-9.75mu\lambda})^{\mu\nu}-\frac{1}{2}\oint_{\partial\Sigma}\bbvar{d}(\delta\bm{S}^{\text{bare}})_{\mu\nu}\,\bbvar{d}(\delta{\mkern 0.8mu\mathchar 22\mkern-9.75mu\lambda})^{\mu\nu}.

(87)

Again, the first term vanishes upon imposing the constraints and we are left with a boundary term for the first-order linearized Lorentz frames. In here, we introduced the
bare
spin current
\delta\bm{S}^{\text{bare}}_{\mu\nu}
, which is conjugate to the internal Lorentz frames. It splits into a background contribution and the contribution for the intrinsic gravitational spin of the perturbation,

\delta\bm{S}_{\mu\nu}^{\text{bare}}=\delta\bm{S}_{\mu\nu}^{o}+\delta\bm{S}_{\mu\nu},

(88)

where

\displaystyle\delta\bm{S}_{\mu\nu}^{o}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{8\pi G}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\tfrac{1}{2}\epsilon_{\mu^{\prime}\nu^{\prime}\rho[\mu}-\gamma^{-1}\eta_{\rho[\mu^{\prime}}\eta_{\nu^{\prime}][\mu}}}\right)\mathrm{d}X^{\mu^{\prime}}_{o}\wedge\mathrm{d}X^{\nu^{\prime}}_{o}\,\mathchoice{\delta{\mkern 0.8mu\mathchar 22\mkern-9.75mu\lambda}^{{{\rho}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.27318pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.27318pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.78163pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.70114pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53337pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.80954pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu]}}}}{\delta{\mkern 0.8mu\mathchar 22\mkern-9.75mu\lambda}^{{{\rho}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.27318pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.27318pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.78163pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.70114pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53337pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.80954pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu]}}}}{\delta{\mkern 0.8mu\mathchar 22\mkern-9.75mu\lambda}^{{{\rho}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.27318pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.27318pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.78163pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.70114pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53337pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.80954pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu]}}}}{\delta{\mkern 0.8mu\mathchar 22\mkern-9.75mu\lambda}^{{{\rho}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.27318pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.27318pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.78163pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.70114pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53337pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.80954pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\nu]}}}}+

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{1}{4\pi G}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\tfrac{1}{2}\epsilon_{\mu\nu\mu^{\prime}\nu^{\prime}}-\gamma^{-1}\eta_{\mu[\mu^{\prime}}\eta_{\nu^{\prime}]\nu}}}\right)\mathrm{d}X^{\mu^{\prime}}_{o}\wedge\mathrm{d}(\delta X^{\nu^{\prime}}),

(89)

\displaystyle\delta\bm{S}_{\mu\nu}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{4\pi G}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\tfrac{1}{2}\epsilon_{\mu\nu\mu^{\prime}\nu^{\prime}}-\gamma^{-1}\eta_{\mu[\mu^{\prime}}\eta_{\nu^{\prime}]\nu}}}\right)\mathrm{d}X^{\mu^{\prime}}_{o}\wedge\delta f^{\nu^{\prime}}.

(90)

We thus find that the covariant phase space for linearized general relativity describes, in addition to gravitational radiation, the linearized reference fields
\delta X^{\mu}
for the diffeomorphism group and
(\delta{\mkern 0.8mu\mathchar 22\mkern-9.75mu\lambda})^{\mu\nu}
for the Lorentz group at the boundary of the spacetime region. Moreover, the explicit form of the symplectic structure (
81
) allows us to infer their canonically conjugate momenta: the momentum current
\delta\bm{P}_{\mu}
and the spin current
\delta\bm{S}_{\mu\nu}
.

IV
Mapping Covariant Reference Frames into the Canonical Phase Space

So far, we left the gauge conditions on the reference fields
X^{\mu}
and
\mathchoice{\Lambda^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}}}}{\Lambda^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}}}}{\Lambda^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}}}}{\Lambda^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}}}}
unspecified. The goal of this section is to choose specific gauge conditions and complete the gauge fixing procedure. More specifically, we will see that the covariant gauge fixing conditions introduced in Section
II
indeed reproduce the geometrical clocks, constructed from the linearized Ashtekar-Barbero variables, which are utilized in
[
31
]
.

IV.1
Choice of clock and spatial reference fields

The starting point is a perturbative expansion on the extended phase space. As mentioned above, we take the background configuration to be spatially flat with vanishing extrinsic curvature. In terms of standard ADM variables, this amounts to setting

\displaystyle h_{ab}

\displaystyle={}^{(0)}h_{ab}+\delta h_{ab}+\mathcal{O}(\delta^{2})\equiv\delta_{ab}+\delta h_{ab}+\mathcal{O}(\delta^{2}),

(91)

\displaystyle\tilde{\pi}^{ab}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\mathrm{det}{}^{(0)}h}\,\delta{\pi}^{ab}+\mathcal{O}(\delta^{2})=\delta{\pi}^{ab}+\mathcal{O}(\delta^{2}),

(92)

where
\delta_{ab}
is the flat Euclidean metric and we removed the density weights from the perturbation of the ADM momentum. Notice that the extrinsic curvature vanishes to zeroth order in the expansion. Thus

\delta{\pi}^{ab}=\delta K^{ab}-\delta^{ab}\delta K,

(93)

where all indices of the perturbation
(\delta h_{ab},\delta K_{ab})
are now raised and lowered with the flat Euclidean reference metric
\delta_{ab}
.

Next, we consider the perturbative expansion of the additional configuration variables that we introduced to build the extended phase space. We write

\displaystyle X^{\mu}

\displaystyle=X^{\mu}_{o}+\delta X^{\mu}+\mathcal{O}(\delta^{2})

(94)

\displaystyle n^{\mu}

\displaystyle=\delta^{\mu}_{0}+\delta n^{\mu}+\mathcal{O}(\delta^{2}),

(95)

\displaystyle t_{\mu}

\displaystyle=-\delta_{\mu}^{0}+\delta t_{\mu}+\mathcal{O}(\delta^{2}).

(96)

To leading order in the expansion, the corresponding canonical momenta, vanish i.e.
(\tilde{P}_{\mu},\tilde{p}_{\mu},\tilde{\Pi}^{\mu})=\mathcal{O}(\delta)
. The zeroth order of the reference frame
X^{\mu}
is adapted to the background metric. We set

X^{0}=t+\delta T+\mathcal{O}(\delta^{2}),\quad X^{i}=x^{i}+\delta X^{i}+\mathcal{O}(\delta^{2})

(97)

where we choose
X^{0}_{o}
such that
\Sigma
is a
t=\mathrm{const}.
surface and
\{x^{i}\}
are Cartesian coordinates thereon. In other words,

\delta_{ab}=\delta_{ij}\mathrm{d}x^{i}_{a}\mathrm{d}x^{j}_{b}.

(98)

In the following,
\partial_{a}
denotes the corresponding Levi Civita covariant derivative. Since
\{x^{i}\}
are Cartesian coordinates, the corresponding Christoffel symbols vanish, i.e.
\partial_{a}\mathrm{d}x^{i}_{b}=\partial_{a}\partial_{b}x^{i}=0
. We will need this equation below.

Next, we consider the gauge fixing conditions at the linearized level. First of all, we set

\delta n^{\mu}=0,\qquad\delta t_{\mu}=0,

(99)

so that the harmonic coordinate condition, namely (
13
) and (
35
) and (
32
), simplifies at leading order to

\delta[\Delta_{h}X^{\mu}]-\delta K\delta^{\mu}_{0}+\mathcal{O}(\delta^{2})=\delta[\Delta_{h}X^{\mu}]+\frac{1}{2}\delta_{ab}\delta{\pi}^{ab}\delta^{\mu}_{0}=0.

(100)

To obtain the first-order harmonic coordinate conditions, we need to take into account the perturbative expansion of the Laplace operator. The spatial metric is perturbed around a flat background,
h_{ab}=\delta_{ab}+\delta h_{ab}
. We obtain

\displaystyle\Delta_{h}

\displaystyle=h^{ab}D_{a}D_{b}=\delta^{ab}\partial_{a}\partial_{b}-\delta h^{ab}\partial_{a}\partial_{b}-\delta^{ab}\,\delta\mathchoice{\Gamma^{{{c}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.85431pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.85431pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.693pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.35213pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1205pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.51463pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{ab}}}}{\Gamma^{{{c}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.85431pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.85431pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.693pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.35213pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1205pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.51463pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{ab}}}}{\Gamma^{{{c}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.85431pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.85431pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.693pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.35213pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1205pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.51463pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{ab}}}}{\Gamma^{{{c}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.85431pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.85431pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.693pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.35213pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1205pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.51463pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{ab}}}}\partial_{c}+\mathcal{O}(\delta^{2}),

(101)

where
\delta h^{ab}\equiv\delta^{ac}\delta^{bd}\delta h_{cd}
, and
\delta\mathchoice{\Gamma^{{{c}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.85431pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.85431pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.693pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.35213pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1205pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.51463pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{ab}}}}{\Gamma^{{{c}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.85431pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.85431pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.693pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.35213pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1205pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.51463pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{ab}}}}{\Gamma^{{{c}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.85431pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.85431pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.693pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.35213pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1205pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.51463pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{ab}}}}{\Gamma^{{{c}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.85431pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.85431pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.693pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.35213pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1205pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.51463pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{ab}}}}
denotes the perturbation of the Christoffel connection. Using that
\delta^{ab}\mathchoice{\Gamma^{{{c}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.85431pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.85431pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.693pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.35213pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1205pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.51463pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{ab}}}}{\Gamma^{{{c}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.85431pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.85431pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.693pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.35213pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1205pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.51463pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{ab}}}}{\Gamma^{{{c}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.85431pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.85431pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.693pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.35213pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1205pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.51463pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{ab}}}}{\Gamma^{{{c}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.85431pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.85431pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.693pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.35213pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1205pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.51463pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{ab}}}}=\partial_{a}\delta h^{ac}-\frac{1}{2}\partial^{c}\delta\mathchoice{h^{{{a}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{a}}}}{h^{{{a}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{a}}}}{h^{{{a}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{a}}}}{h^{{{a}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{a}}}}
with
\delta\mathchoice{h^{{{a}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{a}}}}{h^{{{a}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{a}}}}{h^{{{a}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{a}}}}{h^{{{a}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{a}}}}\equiv\delta^{ac}\delta h_{ac}
. Taking into account
\partial_{a}\partial_{b}x^{i}=0
and
\partial_{a}T=0
, we obtain the linearized gauge fixing conditions

\displaystyle\Delta(\delta T)

\displaystyle=\delta K=-\frac{1}{2}\delta_{ab}\delta{\pi}^{ab},

(102)

\displaystyle\Delta(\delta X^{i})

\displaystyle=\mathrm{d}x^{i}_{b}\,\partial_{a}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\delta h^{ab}-\tfrac{1}{2}\delta^{ab}\mathchoice{\delta h^{{{c}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1205pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.51463pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1205pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.51463pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{c}}}}{\delta h^{{{c}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1205pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.51463pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1205pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.51463pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{c}}}}{\delta h^{{{c}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1205pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.51463pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1205pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.51463pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{c}}}}{\delta h^{{{c}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1205pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.51463pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1205pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.51463pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{c}}}}}}\right),

(103)

where
\Delta=\delta^{ab}\partial_{a}\partial_{b}
is the Laplacian for the flat background metric.
These equations can be inverted with the Green’s function, making the functional dependence between the linearized coordinate fields and the perturbations of the ADM variables explicit. To see that
(\delta T,\delta X^{i})
are, in fact, the same
geometrical clocks
that were constructed earlier in
[
31
]
, we need to switch from the ADM variables
(h_{ab},\tilde{\pi}^{ab})
to the Ashtekar–Barbero variables. The Ashtekar–Barbero variables consist of the densitized triad
\mathchoice{\tilde{E}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{a}}}_{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\tilde{E}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{a}}}_{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\tilde{E}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{a}}}_{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\tilde{E}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{a}}}_{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}
and the Ashtekar–Barbero connection
\mathchoice{A^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{a}}}}{A^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{a}}}}{A^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{a}}}}{A^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{a}}}}
. These variables carry an additional
SU(2)
gauge symmetry, i.e. the rotational subgroup of the internal Lorentz symmetry preserving the internal surface normal
n^{\alpha}=\mathchoice{e^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}}}}{e^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}}}}{e^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}}}}{e^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}}}}n^{\mu}
of
\Sigma
. This internal gauge symmetry is invisible on the ADM phase space. The clock
\delta T
and spatial reference frame
\{\delta X^{i}\}
are invariant under these internal transformations, which is clear given their non-perturbative (
35
) and perturbative definitions (
102
) and (
103
). We can thus choose whatever
SU(2)
gauge we like. To simplify our analysis going forward, we choose a specific
SU(2)
gauge in which

e^{i}=\mathrm{d}x^{i}+\delta e^{i}+\mathcal{O}(\delta^{2}).

(104)

Thus, the background configuration
{}^{(0)}e^{i}
is simply the Euclidean coordinate differentials
\mathrm{d}x^{i}
. Next, we consider the perturbation of the densitized triad (
159
) for given co-triadic perturbation
\delta e^{i}
. First of all, let us recall that it is always possible to rewrite the
densitized triad

\tilde{E}^{~a}_{i}
as

\tilde{E}^{\;\;a}_{i}=\mathrm{d}^{3}x\det(e)\,\mathchoice{e^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{a}}}_{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{e^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{a}}}_{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{e^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{a}}}_{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{e^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{a}}}_{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}=\frac{1}{2}\tilde{\epsilon}^{abc}\epsilon_{ijk}e^{j}_{\;\;b}e^{k}_{\;\;c},

(105)

where
\epsilon_{ijk}
denotes the internal Levi-Civita alternating tensor, i.e. the structure constants of

, while

is the Levi-Civita tensor density. It is then immediate to check that the variation of the co-triad can be determined from the variation of the densitized triad through

(106)

To simplify our notation further, we drop the fiducial density weights

from the linear perturbation of
\mathchoice{\tilde{E}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{a}}}_{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\tilde{E}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{a}}}_{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\tilde{E}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{a}}}_{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\tilde{E}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{a}}}_{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}
, thus writing

\mathchoice{\tilde{E}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{a}}}_{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\tilde{E}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{a}}}_{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\tilde{E}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{a}}}_{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\tilde{E}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{a}}}_{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}=\mathrm{d}^{3}x\,\partial^{a}_{i}+\mathrm{d}^{3}x\,\delta\mathchoice{E^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{a}}}_{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{E^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c (107)

Choice of linearized clock.
Let us now return to the definition of the linearized clock (
102
) and express it as a functional on the phase space of linearized Ashtekar–Barbero variables.
The definition (
102
) contains the trace of the extrinsic curvature. In terms of Ashtekar–Barbero variables, the extrinsic curvature is a derived quantity. Using (
160
), we have

K^{i}_{\;\;a}=\frac{1}{\gamma}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(A^{i}_{\;\;a}-\Gamma^{i}_{\;\;a}}}\right).

(108)

The ADM momentum, which is related to the extrinsic curvature through (
9
), vanishes at zeroth order. At this order, the co-triad is simply given by
{}^{(0)}e^{i}=\mathrm{d}x^{i}
, see (
104
). The corresponding
SU(2)
spin connection coefficients
\Gamma^{i}_{\;\;a}
must clearly vanish, and we obtain

\mathchoice{A^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{a}}}}{A^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{a}}}}{A^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{a}}}}{A^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{a}}}}=\delta\mathchoice{A^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{a}}}}{A^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{a}}}}{A^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{a}}}}{A^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{a}}}}+\mathcal{O}(\delta^{2}).

(109)

To obtain the variation of the trace of the extrinsic curvature, we note

\delta K=\delta(K^{a}_{\;\;a})=\delta(K^{i}_{\;\;a}e_{i}^{\;\;a})=\frac{1}{\gamma}\delta\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(A^{i}_{\;\;a}-\Gamma^{i}_{\;\;a}}}\right)\partial^{a}_{i}.

(110)

To proceed further, we simplify the last term in this expression. Note that

\tilde{E}_{i}^{~a}\Gamma^{i}_{~a}=\frac{1}{2}\tilde{\epsilon}^{abc}\epsilon_{ijk}\mathchoice{\Gamma^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{a}}}}{\Gamma^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{a}}}}{\Gamma^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{a}}}}{\Gamma^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{a}}}}e^{j}_{\;\;b}e^{k}_{\;\;c}=\frac{1}{2}\mathchoice{\epsilon^{{{k}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.54285pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.54285pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.98645pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.84746pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptsc
