e^{j}\wedge e_{k}=-\frac{1}{2}\mathrm{d}e^{k}\wedge e_{k},

(111)

where we used in the last step the torsionless condition for the spatial Levi Civita connection on
\Sigma
, i.e.
De^{i}=\mathrm{d}e^{i}+\mathchoice{\epsilon^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.13373pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.13373pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.00354pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.57394pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{jk}}}}{\epsilon^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.13373pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.13373pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.00354pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.57394pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{jk}}}}{\epsilon^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.13373pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.13373pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.00354pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.57394pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{jk}}}}{\epsilon^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.13373pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.13373pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.00354pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.57394pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{jk}}}}\Gamma^{j}\wedge e^{k}=0
. This implies further that

\delta(\tilde{E}^{\;\;a}_{i}\Gamma^{i}_{\;\;a})=-\frac{1}{2}\tilde{\epsilon}^{abc}\partial_{a}(\delta e_{kb})\mathrm{d}x^{k}_{c}=\frac{1}{2}\mathchoice{\epsilon^{{{ij}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.54285pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.54285pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.98645pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.84746pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{k}}}}{\epsilon^{{{ij}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.54285pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.54285pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.98645pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.84746pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{k}}}}{\epsilon^{{{ij}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.54285pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.54285pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.98645pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.84746pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{k}}}}{\epsilon^{{{ij}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.54285pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.54285pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.98645pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.84746pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{k}}}}\partial^{a}_{i}\partial_{a}(\delta\tilde{E}^{\;\;b}_{j})\mathrm{d}x^{k}_{b}.

(112)

Thus, the defining equation (
102
) for the linearized time coordinate becomes

\Delta(\delta T)=\frac{1}{\gamma}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\partial^{a}_{i}\delta A^{i}_{\;\;a}-\frac{1}{2}\mathchoice{\epsilon^{{{ij}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.54285pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.54285pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.98645pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.84746pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{k}}}}{\epsilon^{{{ij}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.54285pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.54285pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.98645pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.84746pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{k}}}}{\epsilon^{{{ij}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.54285pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.54285pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.98645pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.84746pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{k}}}}{\epsilon^{{{ij}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.54285pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.54285pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.98645pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.84746pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{k}}}}\partial^{a}_{i}\partial_{a}(\delta E^{\;\;b}_{j})\mathrm{d}x^{k}_{b}}}\right).

(113)

We can now solve this gauge condition with the Green’s function
G^{\Delta}
for the flat Laplace operator
\Delta=\delta^{ab}\partial_{a}\partial_{b}
. Our conventions are
\Delta G^{\Delta}(q,q^{\prime})=-\delta^{(3)}(x,y)
. We then have

\displaystyle(\delta T)(q)

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{\gamma}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\frac{1}{2}\partial^{i}_{c}\epsilon_{a}^{\;\;cb}\partial_{b}(\delta E^{\;\;a}_{i}\ast G^{\Delta})(x)+\partial_{i}^{a}(\delta A^{i}_{\;\;a}\ast G^{\Delta})(q)}}\right).

(114)

This equation reproduces the geometrical clock that can be found in (2.49) of
[
31
]
.

Choice of spatial reference frames.
In (
103
), we defined the spatial reference frames at linear order. To determine them in terms of linearized Ashtekar-Barbero variables, notice first that
\delta h_{ab}=2\delta e_{i(a}\mathrm{d}x^{i}_{b)}=2\delta e_{(ab)}
. Going back to (
106
), we thus also have

\delta h_{ab}=\delta_{ab}\mathrm{d}x^{i}_{c}\delta\mathchoice{E^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{c}}}_{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1205pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.51463pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{E^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{c}}}_{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1205pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.51463pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{E^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{c}}}_{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1205pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.51463pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{E^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{c}}}_{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1205pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.51463pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}-2\delta E_{(ab)},

(115)

where
\delta E_{ab}=\mathrm{d}x^{i}_{a}\delta_{cb}\mathchoice{\delta E^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{c}}}_{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1205pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.51463pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\delta E^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{c}}}_{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1205pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.51463pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\delta E^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{c}}}_{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1205pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.51463pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\delta E^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{c}}}_{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1205pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.51463pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}
. This in turn implies with
\delta h^{ab}=\delta^{ac}\delta^{bd}\delta h_{cd}
that

\delta h^{ab}-\frac{1}{2}\delta^{ab}\delta\mathchoice{h^{{{c}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1205pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.51463pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1205pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.51463pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{c}}}}{h^{{{c}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1205pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.51463pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1205pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.51463pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{c}}}}{h^{{{c}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1205pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.51463pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1205pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.51463pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{c}}}}{h^{{{c}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1205pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.51463pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1205pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.51463pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{c}}}}=-2\delta E^{(ab)}+\frac{1}{2}\delta^{ab}\mathrm{d}x^{i}_{c}\delta\mathchoice{E^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{c}}}_{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1205pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.51463pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{E^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{c}}}_{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1205pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.51463pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{E^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{c}}}_{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1205pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.51463pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{E^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{c}}}_{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.57375pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1205pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.51463pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}.

(116)

We insert this equation back into the linearized coordinate condition, i.e. (
103
). We obtain

\Delta(\delta X^{i})=-\mathrm{d}x^{i}_{b}\,\partial_{a}\Big(2\delta E^{k(b}\partial_{k}^{a)}-\frac{1}{2}\delta^{ab}\partial^{k}_{c}\delta E^{\;\;c}_{k}\Big).

(117)

Once again, we can solve this equation for
\delta X^{i}
as a functional on the phase space of linearized Ashtekar-Barbero variables. Using the Green’s function, we obtain

(\delta X^{i})(q)=\Big(\mathrm{d}x^{i}_{b}\partial^{j}_{c}-\frac{1}{2}\mathrm{d}x^{j}_{b}\partial^{i}_{c}+\delta^{ij}\delta_{bc}\Big)(\partial^{c}\delta E^{\;\;b}_{j}\ast G^{\Delta})(q).

(118)

This equation reproduces the geometrical clocks introduced in equation (2.53) of
[
31
]
. Note that by using the Green’s function
G^{\Delta}
to invert the harmonic gauge condition, we are implicitly assuming a specific falloff. Taking into account the asymptotic falloff conditions for the linearized ADM variables,
7
7

7

Note that the right hand side of (
102
) and (
103
) falls off as
r^{-2}
.
we can see that the reference fields
\delta T
and
\delta X^{i}
can have a finite
r\rightarrow\infty
limit for generic configurations on phase space. The resulting boundary values
\delta Q^{\mu}
depend through (
114
) and (
118
) on the gravitational perturbation
(\delta h_{ab},\delta\pi^{ab})
. Returning to the bulk and boundary symplectic structure (
83
), we can see that the linearized reference fields
\delta X^{\mu}
will not drop out completely from the physical phase space. In the bulk, the linearized reference fields are conjugate to the constraints, which vanish on-shell. Hence there is no bulk contribution involving
\delta X^{\mu}
. At the asymptotic boundary, we are in a different situation. The boundary values
\delta Q^{\mu}=\lim_{r\rightarrow\infty}\delta X^{\mu}
, which are functionals on the linearized kinematical ADM phase space, become physical. In other words, they become Dirac observables. Their conjugate variables are the boundary currents given in (
84
). At finite distance, this argument simplifies. At the linearized level, the gauge conditions (
102
) and (
103
) turn into Poisson’s equation with a source that depends on the linearized ADM perturbations
(\delta h_{ab},\delta\pi^{ab})
. If we want to solve these equations in a finite domain
\mathcal{D}
, we need to specify e.g. Dirichlet boundary conditions for the reference fields, i.e.
\delta X^{\mu}\big|_{\partial\mathcal{D}}=\delta Q^{\mu}
. The resulting reference fields depend, therefore, on both the boundary conditions
\delta Q^{\mu}
and the gravitational perturbation, schematically
\delta X^{\mu}=\delta X^{\mu}[\delta h_{ab},\delta\pi^{ab},\delta Q^{\mu}]
. In the interior of
\mathcal{D}
, the reference fields drop from the symplectic current, because the reference fields are conjugate to constraints that vanish on the physical phase space. On the other hand, there is also a boundary contribution to the symplectic two-form (
83
). This boundary contribution turns the boundary data
\delta Q^{\mu}
into elements of the boundary phase space. In this way, boundary data for gauge fixing conditions turn into physical edge modes.

A few further remarks: going back to the definition of the linearized clock, i.e. (
114
) above, we can see that the coordinate frames do not commute among themselves. This is an immediate consequence of the fact that there is a functional dependence on both
\delta\mathchoice{E^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{a}}}_{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{E^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{a}}}_{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{E^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{a}}}_{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{E^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{a}}}_{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}
and
\mathchoice{\delta A^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{a}}}}{\delta A^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{a}}}}{\delta A^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{a}}}}{\delta A^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{a}}}}
. This dependence creates a non-commutativity between
\delta T
and
\Delta X^{i}
, whereas all spatial reference frames
\delta X^{i}
are mutually commuting. In fact,

\big\{\delta T(q),\delta X^{i}(q^{\prime})\big\}=-4\pi G\mathrm{d}x^{i}_{b}\partial^{b}\Delta^{-2}\delta^{(3)}(q,q^{\prime}).

(119)

This non-commutativity extends to the internal reference frames, which are conjugate to the Gauss constraint. In the linearized theory, this non-commutativity can be always removed by shifting the coordinate functions by an auxiliary contribution which is proportional to the constraints, see
[
31
]
for a systematic way to add such terms through a dual observable map to obtain mutually commuting geometrical clocks. This method can be applied order by order in perturbation theory and hence can be used beyond the linearized theory. Here we observe this non-commutativity directly at the level of the geometric clocks.

An alternative way to obtain this result is to calculate the Dirac bracket associated with the gauge fixings for
\delta n^{\mu},\delta t_{\mu}
and
\delta\Phi^{\mu}
. Since
\delta X^{\mu}
and
\delta\tilde{P}_{\mu}
both commute with
\delta n^{\mu},\delta t_{\mu}
and their conjugate momenta, the Dirac bracket associated with these gauge fixings for
\delta X^{\mu}
and
\delta\tilde{P}_{\mu}
coincides with the ordinary Poisson bracket. However, this is no longer the case if we consider the second-class pair
(\delta\Phi^{\mu},\delta{\bf H}^{\mu})
. Neither
\delta X^{\mu}
nor
\delta\tilde{P}^{\mu}
commute with
\delta{\bf H}^{\mu}
and, moreover,
\delta\tilde{P}_{\mu}
does not commute with
\delta X^{\mu}
. Consequently, there are non-trivial additional contributions in the Dirac bracket compared to the Poisson bracket, leading to a modified algebra for
\delta X^{\mu}
and
\delta\tilde{P}_{\mu}
. In particular, we have
\{\delta X^{\mu},\delta X^{\nu}\}\not=0
and expressing this result in terms of Ashtekar-Barbero variables results in the non-commutativity discussed above.

IV.2
Choice of Internal Lorentz Frames

After having introduced a linearized time function
\delta T
and spatial reference frames
\delta X^{i}
, we are left to choose a reference frame for the internal Lorentz symmetries. This amounts to a mere gauge fixing for the spin connection, or, equivalently, a choice of an orthonormal basis
\bm{E}^{\alpha}
in the Lorentz bundle. Going back to (
49
), we first identify the reference frame
\bm{E}^{\alpha}
with the
SO(1,3)
gauge elements

(120)

In here and below, boldface characters are columns, rows or matrices. The dual basis of
\bm{E}^{\alpha}
is

\bm{e}_{\alpha}=\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\begin{smallmatrix}-\Lambda_{\alpha 0}\\
\phantom{+}\Lambda_{\alpha 1}\\
\phantom{+}\Lambda_{\alpha 2}\\
\phantom{+}\Lambda_{\alpha 3}\end{smallmatrix}}}\right)

(121)

The two bases satisfy

\eta_{\alpha\beta}\bm{E}^{\alpha}\otimes\bm{E}^{\beta}=\operatorname{diag}(-1,+1,+1,+1),\quad\bm{e}_{\alpha}\otimes\bm{E}^{\alpha}=\operatorname{id}.

(122)

One possible choice is given by Lorentz gauge, which is the same as to say
8
8

8

The seemingly simpler gauge condition

\square_{g}\bm{E}^{\alpha}=g^{\mu\nu}\nabla_{\mu}\nabla_{\nu}\bm{E}^{\alpha}=0

is inconsistent with the normalization (
122
).

g^{\mu\nu}\nabla_{\mu}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left[\bm{e}_{\alpha}\otimes\nabla_{\nu}\bm{E}^{\alpha}}}\right]\equiv\nabla_{\mu}\bm{\Delta}^{\mu}=0,

(123)

where
\bm{\Delta}_{\mu}
is an
\mathfrak{so}(1,3)
-valued one-form, whose entries are given in (
50
), and
\nabla_{\mu}
is the covariant derivative. It acts on internal Lorentz indices
\alpha,\beta,\dots
through the
SO(1,3)
spin connection and on space-time indices through the Christoffel symbols, but does not mix the columns of
\bm{E}^{\alpha}
, i.e.
\nabla\mathchoice{\Lambda^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}}}}{\Lambda^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}}}}{\Lambda^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}}}}{\Lambda^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}}}}=\mathrm{d}\mathchoice{\Lambda^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}}}}{\Lambda^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}}}}{\Lambda^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}}}}{\Lambda^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}}}}+\mathchoice{A^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\beta}}}}{A^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\beta}}}}{A^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77 {{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}}}}
.

While (
123
) seems like a natural gauge condition from a Lagrangian point of view, it is less so in the Hamiltonian framework, when working with
SU(2)
Ashtekar–Barbero variables. In that case, a better choice was introduced in
[
31
]
. It is easy to check, in fact, that the linearization of the gauge conditions (
123
) does not reproduce the internal reference frames of
[
31
]
. This is so because the internal reference frames of
[
31
]
are constructed out of the linearized Ashtekar–Barbero connection, whereas (
123
) is a functional of only the Lorentz connection.

To translate the perturbative reference frames
\delta\Xi^{i}(x)
of
[
31
]
into our framework, we proceed in two steps. First, we lift the
SU(2)
Ashtekar–Barbero connection, which is otherwise only defined on a spatial hypersurface, into spacetime. Then, we replace in (
123
) the
SO(1,3)
covariant derivative
\nabla_{\mu}
by the four-dimensional lift of the
SU(2)
Ashtekar-Barbero covariant derivative. This construction requires a few additional building blocks. First of all, we introduce an
3+1
-split in internal space
[
47
,
88
]
. We set

h_{\alpha\beta}:=\eta_{\alpha\beta}+n_{\alpha}n_{\beta},\quad\text{and}\quad\epsilon_{\alpha\beta\gamma}:=\epsilon_{\delta\alpha\beta\gamma}n^{\delta}.

(124)

For any Lorentz vector valued
p
-form
V^{\alpha}\in\Omega^{p}(\mathcal{M}:T\mathbb{M})
, we can now introduce the covariant Ashtekar–Barbero exterior derivative, namely

\mathcal{D}V^{\alpha}=\nabla V^{\alpha}-(n^{\alpha}\nabla n_{\beta}-n_{\beta}\nabla n^{\alpha})\wedge V^{\beta}+\gamma\mathchoice{\epsilon^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.16914pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.16914pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.94933pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.53523pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\delta\beta}}}}{\epsilon^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.16914pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.16914pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.94933pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.53523pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\delta\beta}}}}{\epsilon^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.16914pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.16914pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.94933pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.53523pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\delta\beta}}}}{\epsilon^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[8.16914pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[8.16914pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.94933pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.53523pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\delta\beta}}}}\nabla n^{\delta}\wedge V^{\beta}.

(125)

This connection has the following key properties

\displaystyle\mathcal{D}\eta_{\alpha\beta}=0,\qquad\mathcal{D}n^{\alpha}=0.

(126)

Notice also that the pullback of
\mathcal{D}
to
\Sigma
defines the standard
SU(2)
Ashtekar-Barbero connection for a given foliation if we identify
n^{\alpha}\mathchoice{e^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{a}}}_{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{e^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{a}}}_{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{e^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{a}}}_{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{e^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{a}}}_{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}
with the normal vector field orthogonal to each leaf. This is often referred to as
time gauge
in the literature, see e.g.
[
89
,
48
,
88
]
. It is the choice which we also impose here.

The relationship between
\mathcal{D}
and the
SU(2)
Ashtekar-Barbero connection can be made more explicit by introducing the
3+1
split of the reference fields
\bm{E}^{\alpha}
. We set

\bm{E}^{\alpha}=(\mathchoice{\Lambda^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{0}}}}{\Lambda^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{0}}}}{\Lambda^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{0}}}}{\Lambda^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{0}}}},\mathchoice{\Lambda^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}}}}{\Lambda^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\ma (127)

From (
122
), it follows that

n_{\alpha}\mathchoice{\lambda^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}}}}{\lambda^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}}}}{\lambda^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}}}}{\lambda^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}}}}=0,\qquad\eta_{\alpha\beta}\mathchoice{\lambda^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}}}}{\lambda^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\make (128)

The tensor field
\mathchoice{\lambda^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}}}}{\lambda^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}}}}{\lambda^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}}}}{\lambda^{{{\alpha}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}}}}
provides a soldering form (a sort of projector) between the Lorentz bundle and the internal
SU(2)
frame bundle. If, for example,
\varphi:\Sigma\hookrightarrow\mathcal{M}
is the embedding of the spatial hypersurface into spacetime, the soldering forms

, we assume the background to be flat. More specifically, we take it to be flat with respect to both the covariant Ashtekar-Barbero connection as well as the Lorentz connection itself, i.e.
