\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{\mathrm{i}}{8\pi\gamma G}(\gamma+\mathrm{i})\mathchoice{\mathrm{d}X^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.13893pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.13893pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.58055pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.98611pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\bar{C}}}}_{{{(A}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.00002pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.00002pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.00002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.00002pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\mathrm{d}X^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.13893pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.13893pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.58055pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.98611pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\bar{C}}}}_{{{(A}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.00002pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.00002pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.00002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.00002pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\mathrm{d}X^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.13893pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.13893pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.58055pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.98611pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\bar{C}}}}_{{{(A}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.00002pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.00002pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.00002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.00002pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\mathrm{d}X^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.13893pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.13893pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.58055pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.98611pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\bar{C}}}}_{{{(A}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.00002pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.00002pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.00002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.00002pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\wedge f_{B)\bar{C}}.

(179)

In these variables, the linearized Einstein equations are

\displaystyle\mathrm{d}\Delta_{AB}\wedge\mathchoice{\mathrm{d}X^{{{B}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.00002pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.00002pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.00002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.00002pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\bar{A}}}}}{\mathrm{d}X^{{{B}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.00002pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.00002pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.00002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.00002pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\bar{A}}}}}{\mathrm{d}X^{{{B}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.00002pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.00002pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.00002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.00002pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\bar{A}}}}}{\mathrm{d}X^{{{B}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.00002pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.00002pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.00002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.00002pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.383pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.96252pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.83037pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\bar{A}}}}}=0,

(180)

\displaystyle\mathrm{d}f^{A\bar{A}}+\mathchoice{\Delta^{{{A}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.8526pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.75186pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{C}}}}{\Delta^{{{A}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.8526pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.75186pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{C}}}}{\Delta^{{{A}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.8526pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.75186pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{C}}}}{\Delta^{{{A}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.22598pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.8526pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.75186pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.01392pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.675pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.625pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{C}}}}\wedge\mathrm{d}X^{C\bar{A}}+\mathchoice{\bar{\Delta}^{{{\bar{A}}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.00002pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.00002pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.00002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.00002pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.00002pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.00002pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.00002pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.00002pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\bar{C}}}}}{\bar{\Delta}^{{{\bar{A}}\mathchoic (181)

where
\Delta_{AB}=\Delta_{BA}
is the perturbation of the self-dual connection. It is then straightforward to show that

\mathrm{d}\bm{J}_{AB}=0.

(182)

Appendix C
Relation of Boundary Currents to ADM Energy and Momentum

In this appendix, we show that the linearized boundary currents Eqs. (
162
) and (
164
) are equivalent to the negative value of the linearized ADM energy and momentum
[
27
,
91
]
respectively. To see the equivalence for the energies, we first write out (
162
) in terms of
\delta E^{i}_{\;\;a}
,

\displaystyle\delta P_{0}

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{8\pi G}\oint_{\partial\Sigma}\delta\Gamma_{i}\wedge\mathrm{d}X_{o}^{i}=-\frac{1}{8\pi G}\oint_{\partial\Sigma}dS^{c}\epsilon_{c}^{\;\;ab}\delta\Gamma^{i}_{\;\;a}\partial_{bi}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{16\pi G}\oint_{\partial\Sigma}dS^{c}\epsilon_{c}^{\;\;ab}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(-2\epsilon_{b\tilde{c}}^{\;\;\;\;\tilde{b}}\partial^{l}_{[a}\partial_{\tilde{b}]}(\delta E^{\tilde{c}}_{\;\;l})-\epsilon_{b}^{\;\;d\tilde{b}}\partial_{d}^{l}\delta_{a\tilde{c}}\partial_{\tilde{b}}(\delta E^{\tilde{c}}_{\;\;j})+\epsilon_{ba}^{\;\;\;\;\tilde{b}}\partial_{\tilde{b}}(\delta E^{\tilde{c}}_{\;\;l})}}\right)

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{8\pi G}\oint_{\partial\Sigma}dS^{c}\partial^{l}_{c}\partial_{\tilde{c}}(\delta E^{\tilde{c}}_{\;\;l})=-\frac{1}{8\pi G}\oint_{\partial\Sigma}dS^{a}\partial^{l}_{c}(E^{i}_{\;\;a})^{(0)}\partial_{b}(\delta E^{\tilde{c}}_{\;\;l}).

This can be rewritten in terms of the non-weighted triads using
\delta E_{i}^{a}=\underaccent{\tilde}{\epsilon}^{abc}\epsilon_{ijk}\partial^{j}_{b}\delta e^{k}_{c}
. Using this, we find that

\displaystyle\delta P^{0}

\displaystyle=+\frac{1}{8\pi G}\oint_{\partial\Sigma}dS^{a}\partial^{l}_{c}\partial^{i}_{a}\partial_{b}(\epsilon^{bcd}\epsilon_{ijk}\partial^{j}_{c}\delta e^{k}_{d})

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{8\pi G}\oint_{\partial\Sigma}dS^{a}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\partial^{b}(\partial_{bi}\delta e^{i}_{a})-\partial_{a}(\partial^{b}_{i}\delta e^{i}_{b})}}\right)

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{16\pi G}\oint_{\partial\Sigma}dS^{a}(\partial^{b}\delta h_{ab}-\partial_{a}\delta h^{b}_{\;\;b})=-\delta E_{ADM}.

Note that, in going to the last line, we used that
\delta h_{ab}=2\eta_{ij}\partial^{i}_{(a}\delta e^{j}_{b)}
and that the term involving the anti-symmetric part
\delta_{ij}\partial^{i}_{[a}\delta e^{j}_{b]}=0
vanishes. The latter can be seen by encoding the anti-symmetric part in a one-form
\delta\omega
such that
\delta\omega
such that
\epsilon_{acb}\delta\omega^{c}:=\delta_{ij}\partial^{i}_{[a}\delta\mathchoice{e^{{{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.78056pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.78056pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.46402pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.47429pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{b]}}}}{e^{{{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.78056pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.78056pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.46402pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.47429pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{b]}}}}{e^{{{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.78056pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.78056pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.46402pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.47429pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{b]}}}}{e^{{{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.78056pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.78056pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.46402pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.47429pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{b]}}}}
. Then,

\oint_{\partial\Sigma}d^{2}S^{a}\partial^{b}(\delta_{ij}\partial^{i}_{[a}\delta\mathchoice{e^{{{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.78056pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.78056pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.46402pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.47429pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{b]}}}}{e^{{{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.78056pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.78056pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.46402pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.47429pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{b]}}}}{e^{{{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.78056pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.78056pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.46402pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.47429pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{b]}}}}{e^{{{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.78056pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.78056pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.46402pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.47429pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{b]}}}})=\oint_{\partial\Sigma}dS^{a}\epsilon_{abc}\partial^{b}\omega^{c}=\oint_{\partial\Sigma}\mathrm{d}\omega=0,

(183)

Next, let us turn to the momentum given in (
164
), which can be rewritten as

\displaystyle\delta P_{i}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{8\pi G}\oint_{\partial\Sigma}dS^{c}\epsilon_{c}^{\;\;ab}\epsilon_{ijk}\delta K^{j}_{\;\;a}\partial_{b}^{k}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{8\pi G}\oint_{\partial\Sigma}dS^{c}(\partial_{ci}\partial^{a}_{j}-\partial_{cj}\partial^{a}_{i})\delta K^{j}_{\;\;a}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{8\pi G}\oint_{\partial\Sigma}dS^{c}(\partial_{ci}\delta K^{a}_{\;\;a}-\partial^{a}_{i}\delta K_{ca})

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{8\pi G}\oint_{\partial\Sigma}dS^{b}(\delta K_{ab}-(\delta K)\delta_{ab})\partial^{a}_{i}=-\partial^{a}_{i}(\delta P_{\mathrm{ADM}})_{a}.

Note that the fact that the conserved charges are not equivalent to the linearized ADM energy and momentum themselves but rather to their negative value is consistent with our convention for the constraint
\delta\bm{P}_{\mu}+\delta\bm{H}_{\mu}=0
(cf.
[
109
]
).
